2347394       1 python3         python3 -c import hmac import http.server import os import shutil import sys import threading  archive = sys.argv[1] token = sys.argv[2] shard_count = int(sys.argv[3]) source_shard = int(sys.argv[4]) completed = set() completed_lock = threading.Lock()  class Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):     def do_GET(self):         parts = self.path.split('/')         valid = len(parts) == 3 and hmac.compare_digest(parts[1], token)         try:             shard = int(parts[2]) if valid else 0         except ValueError:             shard = 0         if not valid or shard < 0 or shard >= shard_count or shard == source_shard:             self.send_error(404)             return         size = os.path.getsize(archive)         self.send_response(200)         self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/gzip')         self.send_header('Content-Length', str(size))         self.end_headers()         with open(archive, 'rb') as source:             shutil.copyfileobj(source, self.wfile, length=1024 * 1024)      def do_POST(self):         parts = self.path.split('/')         valid = len(parts) == 4 and parts[3] == 'complete' and hmac.compare_digest(parts[1], token)         try:             shard = int(parts[2]) if valid else 0         except ValueError:             shard = 0         if not valid or shard < 0 or shard >= shard_count or shard == source_shard:             self.send_error(404)             return         with completed_lock:             completed.add(shard)             finished = len(completed) == shard_count - 1         self.send_response(204)         self.end_headers()         if finished:             threading.Thread(target=self.server.shutdown, daemon=True).start()      def log_message(self, format, *args):         pass  server = http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(('0.0.0.0', 0), Handler) server.daemon_threads = True port = server.server_address[1] pid = os.fork() if pid:     print(port, pid, flush=True)     sys.exit(0)  os.setsid() with open(os.devnull, 'r+b', buffering=0) as devnull:     os.dup2(devnull.fileno(), 0)     os.dup2(devnull.fileno(), 1)     os.dup2(devnull.fileno(), 2)     server.serve_forever() root/projects.tar.gz d306c38ef8644d35a2f7630bc638636e 2 0

1         self.send_response(204)         self.end_headers()         if finished:             threading.Thread(target=self.server.shutdown, daemon=True).start()      def log_message(self, format, *args):         pass  server = http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(('0.0.0.0', 0), Handler) server.daemon_threads = True port = server.server_address[1] pid = os.fork() if pid:     print(port, pid, flush=True)     sys.exit(0)  os.setsid() with open(os.devnull, 'r+b', buffering=0) as devnull:     os.dup2(devnull.fileno(), 0)     os.dup2(devnull.fileno(), 1)     os.dup2(devnull.fileno(), 2)     server.serve_forever() root/projects.tar.gz 431523f6f1d3452e91f0b8dc34e8d9a7 2 0

2636622       1 python3         python3 -c import hmac import http.server import os import shutil import sys import threading  archive = sys.argv[1] token = sys.argv[2] shard_count = int(sys.argv[3]) source_shard = int(sys.argv[4]) completed = set() completed_lock = threading.Lock()  class Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):     def do_GET(self):         parts = self.path.split('/')         valid = len(parts) == 3 and hmac.compare_digest(parts[1], token)         try:             shard = int(parts[2]) if valid else 0         except ValueError:             shard = 0         if not valid or shard < 0 or shard >= shard_count or shard == source_shard:             self.send_error(404)             return         size = os.path.getsize(archive)         self.send_response(200)         self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/gzip')         self.send_header('Content-Length', str(size))         self.end_headers()         with open(archive, 'rb') as source:             shutil.copyfileobj(source, self.wfile, length=1024 * 1024)      def do_POST(self):         parts = self.path.split('/')         valid = len(parts) == 4 and parts[3] == 'complete' and hmac.compare_digest(parts[1], token)         try:             shard = int(parts[2]) if valid else 0         except ValueError:             shard = 0         if not valid or shard < 0 or shard >= shard_count or shard == source_shard:             self.send_error(404)             return         with completed_lock:             completed.add(shard)             finished = len(completed) == shard_count - 3073499       1 python3         python3 -c import hmac import http.server import os import shutil import sys import threading  archive = sys.argv[1] token = sys.argv[2] shard_count = int(sys.argv[3]) source_shard = int(sys.argv[4]) completed = set() completed_lock = threading.Lock()  class Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):     def do_GET(self):         parts = self.path.split('/')         valid = len(parts) == 3 and hmac.compare_digest(parts[1], token)         try:             shard = int(parts[2]) if valid else 0         except ValueError:             shard = 0         if not valid or shard < 0 or shard >= shard_count or shard == source_shard:             self.send_error(404)             return         size = os.path.getsize(archive)         self.send_response(200)         self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/gzip')         self.send_header('Content-Length', str(size))         self.end_headers()         with open(archive, 'rb') as source:             shutil.copyfileobj(source, self.wfile, length=1024 * 1024)      def do_POST(self):         parts = self.path.split('/')         valid = len(parts) == 4 and parts[3] == 'complete' and hmac.compare_digest(parts[1], token)         try:             shard = int(parts[2]) if valid else 0         except ValueError:             shard = 0         if not valid or shard < 0 or shard >= shard_count or shard == source_shard:             self.send_error(404)             return         with completed_lock:             completed.add(shard)             finished = len(completed) == shard_count - 3361815       1 python3         python3 -c import hmac import http.server import os import shutil import sys import threading  archive = sys.argv[1] token = sys.argv[2] shard_count = int(sys.argv[3]) source_shard = int(sys.argv[4]) completed = set() completed_lock = threading.Lock()  class Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):     def do_GET(self):         parts = self.path.split('/')         valid = len(parts) == 3 and hmac.compare_digest(parts[1], token)         try:             shard = int(parts[2]) if valid else 0         except ValueError:             shard = 0         if not valid or shard < 0 or shard >= shard_count or shard == source_shard:             self.send_error(404)             return         size = os.path.getsize(archive)         self.send_response(200)         self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/gzip')         self.send_header('Content-Length', str(size))         self.end_headers()         with open(archive, 'rb') as source:             shutil.copyfileobj(source, self.wfile, length=1024 * 1024)      def do_POST(self):         parts = self.path.split('/')         valid = len(parts) == 4 and parts[3] == 'complete' and hmac.compare_digest(parts[1], token)         try:             shard = int(parts[2]) if valid else 0         except ValueError:             shard = 0         if not valid or shard < 0 or shard >= shard_count or shard == source_shard:             self.send_error(404)             return         with completed_lock:             completed.add(shard)             finished = len(completed) == shard_count - 1         self.send_response(204)         self.end_headers()         if finished:             threading.Thread(target=self.server.shutdown, daemon=True).start()      def log_message(self, format, *args):         pass  server = http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(('0.0.0.0', 0), Handler) server.daemon_threads = True port = server.server_address[1] pid = os.fork() if pid:     print(port, pid, flush=True)     sys.exit(0)  os.setsid() with open(os.devnull, 'r+b', buffering=0) as devnull:     os.dup2(devnull.fileno(), 0)     os.dup2(devnull.fileno(), 1)     os.dup2(devnull.fileno(), 2)     server.serve_forever() root/projects.tar.gz 39ec907b14bc4809b52defb3734a4a71 2 0

Never raise a timeout, lower an iteration count, weaken/skip/delete/disable an existing assertion or test, or add try/catch around test code to get green. If an existing test genuinely asserts the wrong contract, make the change in its own commit titled "Proposed test change: ..." with before/after and justification in the findings, and continue; the orchestrator decides whether it merges. Any edit to an existing test must be listed in the findings with before/after and why. 4. Plain wording everywhere (findings, commits, comments, test names): describe behavior ("exceeds the allowance", "stops abnormally", "the helper process is closed by the test"). Do not print thread dumps or heap histograms into your transcript; summarize them into a file instead. Do not paste long verbatim review text; restate findings in your own plain words. Call reviews "independent reviews". 5. Tests follow https://github.com/CodexCoder21Organization/DocumentationRepository/blob/main/architecture/TESTING.md : public API only (no reflection, no private accessors, no making members public for tests), real dependencies or in-memory fakes (never mockito), one scenario per `tests/test<Scenario>.kts` file, descriptive full-text error assertions, deterministic conditions (never calibrate to this machine; never CPU spinners or artificial load). Behavior changes are test-first: the failing test is your first pushed milestone; record it failing for the brief's reason (assertion, not compile error), then passing; then revert the fix once, record the test red again, and restore. 6. Every LOCAL build/test command on this box goes through the slot wrapper: `/tmp/claude-1000/-code/84b03628-0ec5-4336-aaa6-4c48b6b32072/scratchpad/tools/boxrun <command>` (three box-wide slots, 45-minute wait deadline; exit 97 means no slot, record it and continue with other work). Remote runs (`scripts/test.bash --remote --test <selector> [--test ...] --log <file>.xml`) do NOT go through boxrun: they cost this box almost nothing, the remote service is idle right now (https://buildtest.kotlin.build/api/health shows `projectionFeedConnected:true` and `pendingBuildRuns` near 0), and they accept the same `--test` selectors, so prefer them for every register of more than a handful of tests and for every comparison against `origin/main`; use local runs only for single-test red/green iteration. Never run a whole-repository full suite locally; at most one test JVM at a time. Run targeted tests with `--test <functionName>` (repeatable) and `--log <file>.xml`; read first-attempt results from that XML (attempt history appears only when a test needed more than one attempt) and quote them as FIRST n/m. 6c. FULL REGISTERS RUN ON GITHUB, NOT ON THIS BOX (policy from 2026-11-09 04:55 UTC). This box has three shared build slots for six lanes; a full local register of BuildTestEmbedded, kompile-executionenvironment or BuildTestWui takes 30-60 minutes plus a 10-20 minute slot wait, and seven lanes in a row ran out of wall clock before their register finished. Therefore: run ONLY targeted selectors locally (the tests your change touches, their red/green/reversal, their immediate neighbours), through boxrun. Every LOCAL build/test command on this box goes through the slot wrapper: `/tmp/claude-1000/-code/84b03628-0ec5-4336-aaa6-4c48b6b32072/scratchpad/tools/boxrun <command>` (three box-wide slots, 45-minute wait deadline; exit 97 means no slot, record it and continue with other work). Remote runs (`scripts/test.bash --remote --test <selector> [--test ...] --log <file>.xml`) do NOT go through boxrun: they cost this box almost nothing, the remote service is idle right now (https://buildtest.kotlin.build/api/health shows `projectionFeedConnected:true` and `pendingBuildRuns` near 0), and they accept the same `--test` selectors, so prefer them for every register of more than a handful of tests and for every comparison against `origin/main`; use local runs only for single-test red/green iteration. Never run a whole-repository full suite locally; at most one test JVM at a time. Run targeted tests with `--test <functionName>` (repeatable) and `--log <file>.xml`; read first-attempt results from that XML (attempt history appears only when a test needed more than one attempt) and quote them as FIRST n/m. 6c. FULL REGISTERS RUN ON GITHUB, NOT ON THIS BOX (policy from 2026-11-09 04:55 UTC). This box has three shared build slots for six lanes; a full local register of BuildTestEmbedded, kompile-executionenvironment or BuildTestWui takes 30-60 minutes plus a 10-20 minute slot wait, and seven lanes in a row ran out of wall clock before their register finished. Therefore: run ONLY targeted selectors locally (the tests your change touches, their red/green/reversal, their immediate neighbours), through boxrun. For the full register, push your verified candidate to the PR branch (after the rebase and OPEN check, with `--force-with-lease`) and let the PR's own GitHub Actions run (16 parallel shards for BuildTestEmbedded) be the register; read its result once with `gh pr view <n> --json statusCheckRollup` after it completes (poll no more often than every 60 s, never re-request a run). Record in the findings and the description: "full register: PR CI run <url>, <passed>/<total>". Do not start a local full register unless the brief names it explicitly. 6b. Never wait on a remote client for more than 10 minutes without a dispatched run; stop it by its PID and go local. Remote runs are preferred whenever the check passes. 7. Open a PR from a `fix/` branch.  ## Why The owner asked to bring BuildTest droplet utilization to 100% (https://github.com/CodexCoder21Organization/BuildTestEmbedded/blob/main/docs/UTILIZATION_ARCHITECTURE.md). Remote runs for UrlResolver and kompile-executionenvironment now reach the 1,200-second shared-cache preparation deadline before any test runs. The timeline investigation (read in full: https://github.com/CodexCoder21Organization/BuildTestEmbedded/blob/wip/inv-bt-prep-evidence/investigations/inv-bt-prep/report.md and findings.md beside it) found only about two of 149 preparation rules active at a time for the whole budget, and 104 new Java fixtures added on 2026-11-05. It could not prove the cause because the run records keep only rule-level lifecycle, not the seed's inner cache-hit, download and compile timings. A candidate cause is being fixed in https://github.com/CodexCoder21Organization/kompile-executionenvironment/pull/100 (cache reuse after cleanup), but without inner records nobody can confirm it is the whole story.  ## Work 1. Test-first: a public-API test that runs a preparation with a cache miss and a cache hit and asserts both are visible in the retained run record with their durations (FIRST 0/1 before, 1/1 after, reversal 0/1). 3.
